- **session-titles**: For AI-DLC projects: keeps every session that runs the workflow titled by what it is on — `&lt;unit&gt; — &lt;stage&gt;` only in a Unit's checkout that really works on the Unit (a Haiku agent judges), else `&lt;phase&gt; — &lt;stage&gt;` (just `Construction` in the main checkout on main; elsewhere on Construction the developer is asked whether it is a Unit checkout); a session opened from a Unit's branch for other work keeps its title. Read from the project's state, never from the agent. Set at each prompt and, in the desktop app, renamed mid-turn when the stage or Unit moves on. ON by default; configurable via SESSION_TTS_ENABLED and SESSION_TTS_VOLUME env vars in settings.json; /session-tts:tts and /session-tts:volume for runtime overrides.
